Continuing work from thread T-019c736c-b9dd-7227-a190-de497ef7f2d0. When you lack specific information you can use read_thread to get it.

@src/recent_files.rs @src/model/mod.rs @src/update/ui.rs

Other relevant files: src/view/mod.rs, src/model/ui.rs, src/update/layout.rs, src/update/app.rs, src/keymap/command.rs, src/view/geometry.rs

* I implemented the Recent Files feature across all 5 phases as defined in docs/feature/recent-files.md.
* I created src/recent_files.rs which handles the persistence of recent files to ~/.config/token-editor/recent.json using serde_json. It maintains an MRU list of up to 50 entries.
* I integrated RecentFiles into AppModel in src/model/mod.rs and added a record_file_opened method that saves changes in a background thread.
* I hooked into file opening logic in src/update/app.rs (AppMsg::FileLoaded), src/update/layout.rs (open_file_in_new_tab), and src/runtime/app.rs (CLI opens) to ensure all opened files are tracked.
* I implemented a new modal (ModalId::RecentFiles) triggered by Cmd+E. I added the necessary state in src/model/ui.rs and message handling in src/update/ui.rs.
* I updated the rendering in src/view/mod.rs to display the recent files list with filenames, relative paths, and "time ago" timestamps.
* I replaced the default font in assets/JetBrainsMono.ttf with a Nerd Font version to support file type icons in the UI.
* I adjusted the modal logic so the currently focused file is excluded from the list, ensuring that Cmd+E followed by Enter immediately swaps to the previously active file.
* I fixed several compilation errors in binaries, benchmarks, and tests caused by adding the recent_files field to AppModel.
* I verified the implementation with 9 unit tests in src/recent_files.rs, which are all passing.
* I noted some potential code duplication between the Recent Files modal and the File Finder modal, particularly in state management and rendering logic.
* I need to ensure comprehensive test coverage, look for further code duplication, and update the CHANGELOG.md.

recent files feature feels nice to use, we can commit thisfeature, lets first ensure we have good test coverage for it, scrutinize our implementation and look for code duplicatioin we might hhave introduced, udpate changelog with the feature

SplitDirection};\n11: use crate::panel::PanelId;\n12: use crate::syntax::LanguageId;\n13: use crate::theme::{load_theme, Theme};\n14: \n15: use super::{update_document, update_layout, update_ui, SYNTAX_DEBOUNCE_MS};\n16: \n17: /// Handle app messages (file operations, window events)\n18: pub fn update_app(model: &mut AppModel, msg: AppMsg) -> Option<Cmd> {\n19:     match msg {\n20:         AppMsg::Resize(width, height) => {\n21:             model.resize(width, height);\n22: \n23:             // Update CSV viewport size if in CSV mode\n24:             if let Some(editor) = model.editor_area.focused_editor_mut() {\n25:                 if let Some(csv) = editor.view_mode.as_csv_mut() {\n26:                     let line_height = model.line_height.max(1);\n27:                     let tab_bar_height = model.metrics.tab_bar_height;\n28:                     let status_bar_height = line_height;\n29:                     let col_header_height = line_height;\n30:                     let content_height = (height as usize)\n31:                         .saturating_sub(tab_bar_height)\n32:                         .saturating_sub(status_bar_height)\n33:                         .saturating_sub(col_header_height);\n34:                     let visible_rows = content_height / line_height;\n35:                     csv.set_viewport_size(visible_rows.max(1), csv.viewport.visible_cols);\n36:                 }\n37:             }\n38: \n39:             Some(Cmd::Redraw)\n40:         }\n41: \n42:         AppMsg::ScaleFactorChanged(scale_factor) => {\n43:             model.set_scale_factor(scale_factor);\n44:             // Reinitialize renderer (creates new glyph cache) and force redraw\n45:             Some(Cmd::Batch(vec![Cmd::ReinitializeRenderer, Cmd::Redraw]))\n46:         }\n47: \n48:         AppMsg::SaveFile => {\n49:             let file_path = model.document().file_path.clone();\n50:             match file_path {\n51: The global splitter\n847: /// index is computed by summing splitter counts during traversal.\n848: ///\n849: /// When the target container is found, the closure `f` is called with:\n850: /// - A fn visit_splitter_container_mut<F>(\n855:     layout: &mut LayoutNode,\n856:     target_index: usize,\n857:     current_index: &mut usize,\n858:     f: &mut F,\n859: ) -> bool\n860: where\n861:     F: => rect.width,\n913:                     SplitDirection::Vertical => rect.height,\n914:                 };\n915:                 return Some((container.ratios.clone(), size));\n916:             }\n917: \n918:             *current_index += splitter_count;\n919: \n920:             // Recurse into children with their calculated rects\n921:             let total_size = match container.direction {\n922:                 SplitDirection::Horizontal => rect.width,\n923:                 SplitDirection::Vertical => rect.height,\n924:             };\n925:             let mut offset = 0.0;\n926: \n927:             for (i, child) in container.children.iter().enumerate() {\n928:                 let ratio = container\n929:                     .ratios\n930:                     .get(i)\n931: child_rect = match container.direction {\n936:                     SplitDirection::Horizontal => {\n937:                         Rect::new(rect.x + offset, rect.y, child_size, rect.height)\n938: }\n957: \n958: /// Update ratios in the container that owns the target splitter.\n959: ///\n960: /// Adjusts the two adjacent ratios (at local_idx and local_idx+1) to new values.\n961: fn update_container_ratios_by_splitter(\n962:     layout: &mut LayoutNode,\n963:     target_index: usize,\n964:     local_idx: usize,\n965:     new_left: f32,\n966:     new_right: f32,\n967: ) -> bool   "content": "1: # Changelog\n2: \n3: All notable changes to rust-editor are documented in this file.\n4: \n5: ---\n6: \n7: ## v0.3.18 - 2026-02-19\n8: \n9: ### Added\n10: - Editing: Auto-surround selection — select text and type `(`, `[`, `{`, `\"`, `'`, or `` ` `` to wrap it (e.g., `hello` → `(hello)`). Works with multi-cursor.\n11: - Editing: Matching bracket highlighting — when cursor is adjacent to `()`, `[]`, or `{}`, both brackets are highlighted with a colored background.\n12: - Config: `auto_surround` setting to enable/disable auto-surround behavior (default: `true`)\n13: - Config: `bracket_matching` setting to enable/disable bracket match highlighting (default: `true`)\n14: - Themes: `bracket_match_background` editor color for customizing bracket highlight appearance\n15: \n16: ### Performance\n17: - Syntax highlighting pipeline rewritten: replaced thread-per-debounce with event-loop deadline timers for lower latency.\n18: - Immediate highlight shifting on edits (InsertNewline, DeleteBackward, Paste, DeleteForward, DeleteWordBackward, DeleteWordForward, DeleteLine) eliminates highlight flashing on keystrokes.\n19: \n20: ### Fixed\n21: - Viewport no longer scrolls unexpectedly when clicking near viewport edges or past the midpoint on newly opened editors.\n22: - Modal layout bugs (geometry, hit-testing) fixed with comprehensive test coverage.\n23: \n24: ### Changed\n25: - Refactored modal layout system: replaced ad-hoc layout code with VStack-based `ModalLayout` for cleaner, testable geometry.\n26: \n27: ---\n28: \n29: ## v0.3.17 - 2026-02-18\n30: \n31: ### Added\n32: - Themes: 5 new builtin themes — Dracula, Catppuccin Mocha, Nord, Tokyo Night, Gruvbox Dark\n33: - Syntax: Added sema-lisp syntax sample file\n34: \n35: ---\n36: \n37: ## v0.3.15 - 2026-01-09\n38: \n39: ### Added\n40: - Preview: HTML file preview support. Opening preview on `.html` files now displays the rendered HTML in the webview pane, reusing the existing markdown preview infrastructure.\n41: - Preview: Local resource loading for HTML preview. Images, CSS, and JS files are now served from the document's directory via a custom `token://` protocol handler.\n42: - Preview: Preview now updates to show the new file when switching tabs (if the new file supports preview), instead of closing.\n43: - API: Added `LanguageId::supports_preview()` method to check if a file type supports live preview.\n44: - API: Added `content_to_preview_html()` function that handles both Markdown and HTML content.\n45: \n46: ---\n47: \n48: ## v0.3.14 - 2026-01-09\n49: \n50: ### Added\n51: - Docs: Updated developer OVERVIEW and archived obsolete docs to reflect recent refactors and module moves.\n52: - Tests: Comprehensive damage computation tests added for redraw/damage logic.\n53: \n54: ### Fixed\n55: - Layout: Editor viewport now correctly shrinks to accommodate dock panels (bottom/right). Fixed issue where dock panels would obscure part of the editor area because the logical viewport didn't account for dock sizes.\n56: - Layout: Viewport dimensions now recalculated when dock visibility or size changes.\n57: - Preview: Fixed dual rendering issue where both native markdown and webview were drawn. Native rendering is now properly disabled when webview is active.\n58: - Preview: Fixed webview misalignment by correctly converting physical pixel coordinates to logical points with proper Y-axis flipping for macOS.\n59: - Scroll: Scroll events over dock panels and preview panes are now properly consumed instead of bleeding into the editor.\n60: - Rendering: Minor fixes to selection/cursor rendering behavior related to the cursor-blink optimization and partial redraws.\n61: - Sidebar: Fixed syntax highlighting not triggering when opening files via sidebar double-click. Commands from update() calls in mouse handlers are now properly propagated.\n62: \n63: ### Changed\n64: - Refactor (mouse): Unified mouse event handling with new hit-test system in `src/view/hit_test.rs` and centralized dispatch in `src/runtime/mouse.rs`. Replaces ad-hoc if/else chains with explicit `HitTarget` enum and priority-ordered hit-testing.\n65: - Refactor (app): Introduced specific redraw helpers and tighter damage accumulation to reduce unnecessary full redraws; clearer separation between damage computation and command processing.\n66: - Refactor (layout): Added `redraw_editor`-style helpers to limit redraw scope when layout changes are localized.\n67: - Refactor (ui): Optimized cursor-blink related redraw behavior to prevent selection flicker and avoid unnecessary redraws of unrelated regions.\n68: - Cleanup: Removed dead code from view module (`is_in_tab_bar`, `is_in_modal`, unused Renderer getters, wrapper functions superseded by ViewportGeometry).\n69: \n70: ---\n71: \n72: ## v0.3.13 - 2026-01-07\n73: \n74: ### Fixed - CSV Cell Editing\n75: \n76: **Column Truncation:**\n77: - Fixed column truncation issue during CSV cell editing\n78: - Cells now properly handle content that exceeds initial column width\n79: - No data loss when editing longer values\n80: \n81: **Horizontal Scrolling:**\n82: - Added horizontal scroll support for CSV cell editing\n83: - Long cell content now scrollable within the edit field\n84: - Improved UX for editing cells with long text values\n85: \n86: **Log File Discovery:**\n87: - Fixed log file discovery to find most recent dated log file\n88: - Improved config path resolution for logging\n89: \n90: ### Added - Command Palette Enhancements\n91: \n92: **New Commands:**\n93: - Added \"Open Folder...\" command to open workspace directories\n94: - Added \"Quit\" command (⌘Q) to close the application\n95: - Added \"Toggle Performance Overlay\" command (F2) - debug builds only\n96: - Added \"Toggle Debug Overlay\" command (F8) - debug builds only\n97: \n98: **Implementation:**\n99: - Added `Cmd::Quit` for proper application exit flow\n100: - Debug commands use conditional compilation (`#[cfg(debug_assertions)]`)\n101: - Added `DEBUG_COMMANDS` array for debug-only command definitions\n102: \n103: ### Added - Configuration and Command System\n104: \n105: **Configuration Reload:**\n106: - Implemented `ReloadConfiguration` command for hot-reloading config without restart\n107: - Command palette now includes \"Reload Configuration\" command\n108: - Allows changes to config.yaml and keymap.yaml to take effect immediately\n109: \n110: **Application Commands:**\n111: - Added `ApplicationCommand` enum for app-level operations (quit, reload config)\n112: - Added `WorkspaceCommand` enum for workspace operations (toggle sidebar, refresh, etc.)\n113: - Unified command structure with `Cmd::Application` and `Cmd::Workspace` variants\n114: \n115: **Theme Picker Enhancements:**\n116: - Implemented theme preview with live updates\n117: - Theme changes are applied immediately while browsing\n118: - Restore original theme on cancel (Escape)\n119: - Confirm theme selection with Enter\n120: - Updated theme picker tests for new preview/restore API\n121: \n122: **Configuration Integration:**\n123: - Cursor blink interval now configurable via `editor.cursor_blink_interval_ms` in config.yaml\n124: - Performance overlay toggle now configurable via `editor.show_perf_overlay` in config.yaml\n125: - Runtime respects user configuration preferences\n126: \n127: ### Fixed - Rendering Bugs\n128: \n129: **Selection Highlights Disappearing:**\n130: - Selection highlights were disappearing after ~1 second due to cursor blink optimization\n131: - `render_cursor_lines_only()` now properly renders selection highlights and rectangle selections\n132: - Cursor blink interval changed from 500ms to 600ms\n133: \n134: **CSV Cell Editor Cursor:**\n135: - Fixed cursor appearing at top of editor instead of inside cell when editing CSV\n136: - Skip cursor-lines-only optimization for CSV mode (falls back to full render)\n137: \n138: ### Changed - Build System\n139: \n140: **Cargo.toml Enhancements:**\n141: - Added package metadata: authors, readme, keywords, categories, exclude\n142: - Added `default-run = \"token\"` to handle multiple binaries\n143: - New build profiles:\n144:   - `dev`: Fast compile with `debug = \"line-tables-only\"`, no debug info for deps\n145:   - `debugging`: Full debug info for debuggers\n146:   - `release`: Thin LTO, `panic = \"abort\"` for local testing\n147:   - `dist`: Fat LTO, `codegen-units = 1`, stripped for distribution\n148:   - `profiling`: Debug symbols, no LTO for flamegraph/samply\n149: \n150: **Makefile Updates:**\n151: - Added `make dist` and `make debugging` targets\n152: - Cross-compilation targets now use `dist` profile for maximum optimization\n153: - `make bundle-macos` uses `dist` profile for smallest binary\n154: - Updated help text\n155: \n156: ---\n157: \n158: ## v0.3.12 - 2025-12-20\n159: \n160: ### Added - Find/Replace Implementation\n161: \n162: Complete implementation of the Find/Replace modal (Cmd+F):\n163: \n164: **Core Search Functions:**\n165: - `find_all_occurrences_with_options()` with case sensitivity support\n166: - `find_next_occurrence_with_options()` for forward search with wrapping\n167: - `find_prev_occurrence_with_options()` for backward search with wrapping\n168: - Full Unicode support (Greek, Japanese, emoji, accented characters)\n169: - Overlapping match detection\n170: \n171: **New Modal Messages:**\n172: - `ToggleFindReplaceField` - Tab to switch between query/replace fields\n173: - `ToggleFindReplaceCaseSensitive` - Toggle case-sensitive search\n174: - `FindNext` / `FindPrevious` - Navigate through matches\n175: - `ReplaceAndFindNext` - Replace current match and find next\n176: - `ReplaceAll` - Replace all occurrences at once\n177: \n178: **UX Improvements:**\n179: - Query persists when reopening Cmd+F (like command palette)\n180: - Transient messages show \"No matches found\" or \"Replaced N occurrences\"\n181: - Selection highlights the current match\n182: - Cursor scrolls to ensure match is visible\n183: \n184: **Test Coverage:**\n185: - 25+ new tests for find functionality\n186: - Case sensitivity tests\n187: - Unicode edge cases (emoji, CJK, Greek letters)\n188: - Overlapping pattern matching\n189: - Empty document and needle handling\n190: \n191: ---\n192: \n193: ## v0.3.11 - 2025-12-20\n194: \n195: ### Fixed - Event Loop Performance\n196: \n197: Critical fix for the event loop spinning issue that caused ~7 FPS in multi-split scenarios:\n198: \n199: **Root Cause:** `ControlFlow::Poll` was spinning the event loop constantly at ~100% CPU even when idle.\n200: \n201: **Fix:** Changed to `ControlFlow::WaitUntil` in `src/runtime/app.rs`:\n202: - Event loop now sleeps until the next cursor blink timer (500ms)\n203: - Wakes immediately for user input, async messages, or file system changes\n204: - Idle CPU usage dropped from ~100% to ~0%\n205: - Multi-split FPS improved from ~7 to 60 (VSync limited)\n206: \n207: **Performance Profile (30-second live session):**\n208: | Category | Time |\n209: |----------|------|\n210: | Idle/Waiting | 77.6% |\n211: | Event Handling | 21.5% |\n212: | Rendering | ~0.9% |\n213: \n214: ### Fixed - Debug Overlay HiDPI Scaling\n215: \n216: Fixed hard-coded pixel values in the performance overlay (`src/runtime/perf.rs`) that didn't scale on Retina displays:\n217: \n218: - Overlay dimensions now scale based on `line_height` ratio\n219: - Chart widths derived from approximate character width\n220: - Stacked bar calculation fixed with `saturating_sub` to prevent overflow panic\n221: \n222: ### Added - Profiling Documentation\n223: \n224: Enhanced [docs/profiling-guide.md](profiling-guide.md) with recommended workflow:\n225: \n226: - Step-by-step process: headless benchmark → sample command → Instruments\n227: - Interpreting `sample` output (mach_msg2_trap, CFRunLoop patterns)\n228: - Common issues & solutions troubleshooting table\n229: - Example healthy profile output\n230: \n231: ### Changed\n232: \n233: - Archived detailed allocation analysis to [performance-analysis-v1.md](archived/performance-analysis-v1.md)\n234: - Created new [performance-analysis.md](archived/performance-analysis.md) with summary of all optimizations\n235: \n236: ---\n237: \n238: ## v0.3.10 - 2025-12-19\n239: \n240: ### Added - File System Watcher & New Languages\n241: \n242: **Workspace File Watching:**\n243: - Integrated `notify` crate for real-time file system monitoring\n244: - `FileSystemChange` event for workspace updates\n245: - Automatic refresh when files change externally\n246: \n247: **New Language Support:**\n248: - **Scheme** - tree-sitter-scheme parser and highlighting\n249: - **INI** - tree-sitter-ini parser and highlighting  \n250: - Clicks on status bar no longer propagate to editor\n1400: - `Renderer::is_in_status_bar(y)` method for hit testing\n1401: \n1402: ### Changed\n1403: \n1404: - Test count: 246 (was 227)\n1405: - Added 8 delete line tests, 4 duplicate tests, 12 resize tests\n1406: \n1407: ---\n1408: \n1409: ## 2025-12-06\n1410: \n1411: ### Added - Status Bar System\n1412: \n1413: - Structured, segment-based status bar per `docs/feature/STATUS_BAR.md`\n1414: - `StatusBar`, `StatusSegment`, `SegmentId`, `SegmentContent` types\n1415: - `sync_status_bar()` auto-updates segments from model state\n1416: - `StatusBarLayout` for rendering with separator positions\n1417: - Transient messages with auto-expiry (`TransientMessage`)\n1418: - Left/right segment alignment with separators\n1419: - 47 new status bar tests (`tests/status_bar.rs`)\n1420: \n1421: ### Added - Overlay System\n1422: \n1423: - Reusable overlay rendering module (`src/overlay.rs`)\n1424: - `OverlayAnchor` enum (TopLeft, TopRight, BottomLeft, BottomRight, Center)\n1425: - `OverlayConfig` with builder pattern for configuration\n1426: - `render_overlay_background()` with alpha blending\n1427: - `render_overlay_border()` for optional 1px borders\n1428: - `blend_pixel()` for ARGB alpha compositing\n1429: - 7 overlay unit tests\n1430: \n1431: ### Added - Overlay Theme Integration\n1432: \n1433: - `OverlayTheme` with themed colors: background, foreground, highlight, warning, error, border\n1434: - `OverlayThemeData` for YAML parsing (all fields optional for backward compatibility)\n1435: - Perf overlay now uses theme colors instead of hardcoded values\n1436: - Optional border rendering when theme specifies border color\n1437: - Added overlay sections to all 4 theme files\n1438: \n1439: ### Fixed\n1440: \n1441: - Status bar separator lines now span full height (was inset 4px)\n1442: - Direction-aware scroll reveal with `ScrollRevealMode` enum\n1443: - `ensure_cursor_visible_with_mode()` primitive for scroll behavior\n1444: - Arrow key viewport snap-back behavior\n1445: - MoveCursor now properly calls ensure_cursor_visible()\n1446: - Directional reveal: Up→TopAligned, Down→BottomAligned for natural UX\n1447: \n1448: ### Changed\n1449: \n1450: - Test count: 227 (was 185)\n1451: - Added 11 scroll reveal tests, 47 status bar tests, 7 overlay tests\n1452: \n1453: ---\n1454: \n1455: ## 2025-12-05\n1456: \n1457: ### Added - Selection & Multi-Cursor (Phase 7)\n1458: \n1459: #### Phase 7.1: Basic Selection\n1460: \n1461: - Theme support for `selection_background` and `secondary_cursor_color`\n1462: - ~25 new EditorMsg variants for selection/multi-cursor operations\n1463: - Shift+Arrow extends selection, Shift+Home/End, Shift+Click\n1464: - Selection rendering with blue highlight\n1465: - Escape clears selection or collapses multi-cursor\n1466: \n1467: #### Phase 7.2: Selection Editing\n1468: \n1469: - `delete_selection()` helper for selection range deletion\n1470: - InsertChar/InsertNewline deletes selection before inserting\n1471: - DeleteBackward/DeleteForward deletes selection instead of single char\n1472: \n1473: #### Phase 7.3: Word & Line Selection\n1474: \n1475: - SelectWord handler using `char_type` for word boundaries\n1476: - SelectLine handler (selects entire line including newline)\n1477: - Double-click selects word, triple-click selects line\n1478: - Click count tracking with wrap at 4\n1479: \n1480: #### Phase 7.4: Multi-Cursor Basics\n1481: \n1482: - `toggle_cursor_at()` in EditorState\n1483: - ToggleCursorAtPosition handler for Cmd+Click\n1484: - Multi-cursor rendering (primary=white, secondary=semi-transparent)\n1485: \n1486: #### Phase 7.5: Multi-Cursor Editing\n1487: \n1488: - `cursors_in_reverse_order()` helper\n1489: - InsertChar/InsertNewline at all cursors in reverse order\n1490: - DeleteBackward/DeleteForward at all cursors in reverse order\n1491: \n1492: #### Phase 7.6: Clipboard\n1493: \n1494: - arboard dependency for clipboard support\n1495: - Copy (Cmd+C) - copies selection or entire line\n1496: - Cut (Cmd+X) - copies and deletes selection\n1497: - Paste (Cmd+V) - multi-cursor aware, line-per-cursor distribution\n1498: \n1499: #### Phase 7.7: Rectangle Selection\n1500: \n1501: - `RectangleSelectionState` in EditorState\n1502: - Middle mouse down starts rectangle mode\n1503: - Mouse drag updates rectangle, mouse up finishes\n1504: - Creates cursors/selections for each line in rectangle\n1505: - Ghost cursor preview during drag\n1506: \n1507: #### Phase 7.8: AddCursorAbove/Below\n1508: \n1509: - Selection helper methods: `extend_to`, `collapse_to_start/end`, `contains`\n1510: - `deduplicate_cursors()` removes duplicate positions\n1511: - `assert_invariants()` for debug builds\n1512: - AddCursorAbove/Below handlers with column preservation\n1513: - Option+Option+Arrow double-tap detection (300ms threshold)\n1514: \n1515: ### Changed\n1516: \n1517: - Moved 101 tests to tests/ folder (8 remaining in main.rs)\n1518: - Total test count: 185 (10 theme + 11 keyboard + 164 integration)\n1519: \n1520: ---\n1521: \n1522: ## 2025-12-04\n1523: \n1524: ### Added - Architecture Refactoring (Phases 1-6)\n1525: \n1526: #### Phase 1: Split Model\n1527: \n1528: - Created `model/` module hierarchy\n1529: - `Document` struct (buffer, undo/redo, file_path)\n1530: - `EditorState` struct (cursor, viewport)\n1531: - `UiState` struct (status, cursor blink)\n1532: - `AppModel` struct composing all state\n1533: \n1534: #### Phase 2: Nested Messages\n1535: \n1536: - `Direction` enum (Up, Down, Left, Right)\n1537: - `EditorMsg`, `DocumentMsg`, `UiMsg`, `AppMsg` enums\n1538: - `std::thread` + `mpsc` for async operations\n1545: - `process_cmd()` and `process_async_messages()` in event loop\n1546: \n1547: #### Phase 4: Theming\n1548: \n1549: - `src/theme.rs` with Color, Theme, YAML parsing\n1550: - All hardcoded colors replaced with theme lookups\n1551: - 6 new theme tests (96 total at this point)\n1552: \n1553: #### Phase 5: Multi-Cursor Prep\n1554: \n1555: - `Position` and `Selection` types in editor.rs\n1556: -   "content": "1: # Recent Files\n2: \n3: Persistent recent files list with quick access menu and integration with Quick Open.\n4: \n5: > **Status:** Planned\n6: > **Priority:** P1\n7: > **Effort:** M\n8: > **Created:** 2025-12-19\n9: > **Milestone:** 1 - Navigation\n10: \n11: ---\n12: \n13: ## Table of Contents\n14: \n15: 1. [Overview](#overview)\n16: 2. [Architecture](#architecture)\n17: 3. [Data Structures](#data-structures)\n18: 4. [Keybindings](#keybindings)\n19: 5. [Implementation Plan](#implementation-plan)\n20: 6. [Testing Strategy](#testing-strategy)\n21: 7. [References](#references)\n22: \n23: ---\n24: \n25: ## Overview\n26: \n27: ### Current State\n28: \n29: The editor has no memory of previously opened files. Each session starts fresh with only CLI-provided files. Users must navigate the file tree or use file dialog to reopen frequently used files.\n30: \n31: ### Goals\n32: \n33: 1. **Track recently opened files** - Remember files opened via any method\n34: 2. **Persistent storage** - Save list to `~/.config/token-editor/recent.json`\n35: 3. **Quick access menu** - Dedicated \"Open Recent\" submenu or modal\n36: 4. **Quick Open integration** - Show recent files first in Cmd+P results\n37: 5. **Workspace-aware** - Track both workspace-relative and absolute paths\n38: 6. **Cross-session** - Recent files persist across editor restarts\n39: \n40: ### Non-Goals\n41: \n42: - Session restore (reopening all tabs from last session)\n43: - File pinning/favorites (handled in command palette enhancement)\n44: - Recent workspaces/folders (separate tracking)\n45: - Undo close tab (separate feature)\n46: \n47: ---\n48: \n49: ## Architecture\n50: \n51: ```\n52: ┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n53: │                        Recent Files System                                   │\n54: ├─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤\n55: │ CLI args  │  │  File     │  │  Quick    │  │  Drag & Drop      │   │ │\n61: │  │  │           │  │  Dialog   │  │  Open     │  │                   │   │ │\n62: │  │  └─────┬─────┘  └─────┬─────┘        ┌────────────────────┐                               │ │\n68: │  │                   │  RecentFiles.add() │                               │ │\n69: │  │                   └──────────┬─────────┘ │\n72: │                                 │                                            │\n73: │                                 ▼                                            │\n74: │  ┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐ │\n75: │  │                         RecentFiles State                               │ │\n76: │  │ \"/Users/dev/project/src/main.rs\",                       │ │\n98: │  │        \"opened_at\": 1702987654,                                         │ │\n99: │  │        \"workspace\": \"/Users/dev/project\" },                             │ │\n100: │  │      ...                                                                │ │\n101: │  │    ] └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘\n106: ```\n107: \n108: ### Integration Points\n109: \n110: ```\n111: ┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n112: │                     Consumer Integrations                        │\n113: ├─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤\n114: │                                                                  │\n115: │  ┌──────────────────┐    ┌──────────────────┐ when empty      │    │                  │                   │\n122: │  └────────┬─────────┘    └────────┬─────────┘                   │\n123: │           │                       │ src/recent_files.rs\n143: \n144: use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};\n145: use std::path::PathBuf;\n146: use std::time::SystemTime;\n147: \n148: /// A single entry in the recent files list\n149: #[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]\n150: pub struct RecentEntry {\n151:     /// Absolute path to the file\n152:     pub path: PathBuf,\n153:     /// Timestamp when last opened (Unix epoch seconds)\n154:     pub opened_at: u64,\n155:     /// Workspace root when file was opened (if any)\n156:     /// Used to show relative paths in the same workspace\n157:     #[serde(skip_serializing_if = \"Option::is_none\")]\n158:     pub workspace: Option<PathBuf>,\n159:     /// Number of times file has been opened (for ranking)\n160:     #[serde(default)]\n161:     pub open_count: u32,\n162: }\n163: \n164: impl RecentEntry {\n165:     /// Create a new entry for the current time\n166:     pub fn new(path: PathBuf, workspace: Option<PathBuf>) -> Self {\n167:         Self {\n168:             path,\n169:             opened_at: now_epoch_secs(),\n170:             workspace,\n171:             open_count: 1,\n172:         }\n173:     }\n174: \n175:     /// Update entry for re-opening\n176:     pub fn touch(&mut self) {\n177:         self.opened_at = now_epoch_secs();\n178:         self.open_count += 1;\n179:     }\n180: \n181:     /// Get display path (relative to workspace if available, otherwise filename)\n182:     pub fn display_path(&self) -> String {\n183:         if let Some(ws) = &self.workspace {\n184:             if let Ok(relative) = self.path.strip_prefix(ws) {\n185:                 return relative.to_string_lossy().to_string();\n186:             }\n187:         }\n188:         self.path\n189:             .file_name()\n190:             .map(|n| n.to_string_lossy().to_string())\n191:             .unwrap_or_else(|| self.path.to_string_lossy().to_string())\n192:     }\n193: \n194:     /// Get human-readable time since opened\n195:     pub fn time_ago(&self) -> String {\n196:         let now = now_epoch_secs();\n197:         let diff = now.saturating_sub(self.opened_at);\n198: \n199:         if diff < 60 {\n200:             \"just now\".to_string()\n201:         } else if diff < 3600 {\n202:             let mins = diff / 60;\n203:             format!(\"{} min{} ago\", mins, if mins == 1 { \"\" } else { \"s\" })\n204:         } else if diff < 86400 {\n205:             let hours = diff / 3600;\n206:             format!(\"{} hour{} ago\", hours, if hours == 1 { \"\" } else { \"s\" })\n207:         } else if diff < 604800 {\n208:             let days = diff / 86400;\n209:             format!(\"{} day{} ago\", days, if days == 1 { \"\" } else { \"s\" })\n210:         } else {\n211:             let weeks = diff / 604800;\n212:             format!(\"{} week{} ago\", weeks, if weeks == 1 { \"\" } else { \"s\" })\n213:         }\n214:     }\n215: \n216:     /// Check if file still exists\n217:     pub fn exists(&self) -> bool {\n218:         self.path.exists()\n219:     }\n220: }\n221: \n222: fn now_epoch_secs() -> u64 {\n223:     SystemTime::now()\n224:         .duration_since(SystemTime::UNIX_EPOCH)\n225:         .map(|d| d.as_secs())\n226:         .unwrap_or(0)\n227: }\n228: ```\n229: \n230: ### Recent Files Manager\n231: \n232: ```rust\n233: // src/recent_files.rs\n234: \n235: use std::collections::HashSet;\n236: \n237: /// Maximum number of entries to keep\n238: const MAX_ENTRIES: usize = 50;\n239: \n240: /// Persistent recent files list\n241: #[derive(Debug, Clone, Default, Serialize, Deserialize)]\n242: pub struct RecentFiles {\n243:     /// Schema version for forward compatibility\n244:     #[serde(default)]\n245:     pub version: u32,\n246:     /// Recent file entries, most recent first\n247:     pub entries: Vec<RecentEntry>,\n248: }\n249: \n250: impl RecentFiles {\n[... omitted lines 251 to 426 ...]\n427:                 .filter(|e| e.display_path().to_lowercase().contains(&filter_lower))\n428:                 .collect()\n429:         }\n430:     }\n431: \n432:     /// Get selected file path\n433:     pub fn selected_path(&self) -> Option<&PathBuf> {\n434:         let filtered = self.filtered_entries();\n435:         filtered.get(self.selected_index).map(|e| &e.path)\n436:     }\n437: }\n438: \n439: /// Add to ModalId\n440: #[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]\n441: pub enum ModalId {\n442:     // ... existing variants ...\n443:     RecentFiles, // NEW\n444: }\n445: \n446: /// Add to ModalState\n447: pub enum ModalState {\n448:     // ... existing variants ...\n449:     RecentFiles(RecentFilesState), // NEW\n450: }\n451: ```\n452: \n453: ---\n454: \n455: ## Keybindings\n456: \n457: | Action | Mac | Windows/Linux | Notes |\n458: |--------|-----|---------------|-------|\n459: | Open Recent Files | Cmd+E | Ctrl+E | Dedicated recent modal |\n460: | Navigate up | Up Arrow | Up Arrow | Select previous |\n461: | Navigate down | Down Arrow | Down Arrow | Select next |\n462: | Open file | Enter | Enter | Open selected file |\n463: | Open in split | Cmd+Enter | Ctrl+Enter | Open in new split |\n464: | Remove from list | Delete | Delete | Remove selected entry |\n465: | Clear all | Cmd+Shift+Delete | Ctrl+Shift+Delete | Clear entire list |\n466: | Close | Escape | Escape | Close modal |\n467: \n468: Note: Cmd+P (Quick Open) also shows recent files when query is empty.\n469: \n470: ---\n471: \n472: ## Implementation Plan\n473: \n474: ### Phase 1: Core Data Strategy\n554: \n555: ### Unit Tests\n556: \n557: ```rust\n558: #[cfg(test)]\n559: mod tests {\n560:     use super::*;\n561:     use tempfile::tempdir;\n562: \n563:     #[test]\n564:     fn test_add_and_retrieve() {\n565:         let mut recent = RecentFiles::default();\n566:         let path = PathBuf::from(\"/test/file.rs\");\n567: \n568:         recent.add(path.clone(), None);\n569: \n570:         assert_eq!(recent.entries.len(), 1);\n571:         assert_eq!(recent.entries[0].path, path);\n572:     }\n573: \n574:     #[test]\n575:     fn test_mru_ordering() {\n576:         let mut recent = RecentFiles::default();\n577: \n578:         recent.add(PathBuf::from(\"/first.rs\"), None);\n579:         std::thread::sleep(std::time::Duration::from_millis(10));\n580:         recent.add(PathBuf::from(\"/second.rs\"), None);\n581: \n582:         // Most recent first\n583:         assert_eq!(recent.entries[0].path, PathBuf::from(\"/second.rs\"));\n584: Committed. Here's a summary:

**Tests added** (9 → 23 total): Serialization round-trip, workspace update/preservation on reopen, capacity ordering, all `time_ago` ranges (singular/plural), edge case paths, `find_index`, `RecentFilesState` filtering (including case-insensitive), current-file exclusion, and empty state.

This is the correct level of sharing — the modal message handlers in `update/ui.rs` are inherently per-variant matches and can't be further reduced without over-abstracting.

**Changelog** updated with the feature entry. Feature doc status set to **Done (Phases 1–5)**.

## Assistant

Found it. The icons are Nerd Font codepoints in `FileExtension::icon()`. The issue is these are **invisible/empty-looking strings** — they're Nerd Font glyphs but most of them render as blank boxes or nothing if the font doesn't contain those codepoints.

developer targeted fonts with a high number of glyphs (icons). Specifically to add a high number of extra glyphs from popular ‘iconic fonts’ such as [Font Awesome](https://github.com/FortAwesome/Font-Awesome) , [Devicons](http://vorillaz.github.io/devicons/) , [Octicons](https://github.com/github/octicons) , and others.\n\n[](https://github.com/ryanoasis/nerd-fonts)\n\n<sub>_Diagram created using [SankeyMATIC](http://sankeymatic.com/ \"SankeyMATIC (BETA): A Sankey diagram builder for everyone\")_</sub>\n\n##### Features\n\n# Features\n\n## All the icons!\n\n### 10390+ icons combined from popular sets\n\n**.css** **.js**  \n**.html** **.json**  \n**Gruntfile.json** react.jsx  \n**angular.js** **jquery.js** **.md**  \n**git** **php** **.sh**  \n**png** **lhs**  \n**node\\_modules**  \n**.git**\n\n#### Search Icons Cheat Sheet\n\n## The best developer fonts\n\n### 68 + patched and ready to use programming fonts\n\n* Hack\n* FiraCode\n* Meslo\n* Source Code Pro\n* Terminus\n* Monoid\n* Noto\n* Iosevka\n* and many more ...\n\n#### Downloads\n\n## Cross Platform\n\n### Ready to use on any OS platforms and web\n\n## Supported in major projects\n\n### Supported out of the box by many projects\n\n[#### Powerlevel9k](https://github.com/Powerlevel9k/powerlevel9k/)\n\nThe most awesome Powerline theme for ZSH around!\n\n### [colorls](https://github.com/athityakumar/colorls)\n\nA Ruby gem that beautifies the terminal's ls command, with color and font-awesome icons\n\n[#### The Fish Shell Framework](https://github.com/oh-my-fish/oh-my-fish)\n\nOh My Fish provides core infrastructure to allow you to install packages which extend or modify the look of your shell. It's fast, extensible and easy to use.\n\n[#### VimDevIcons](https://github.com/ryanoasis/vim-devicons)\n\nAdds Icons to your Vim Plugins\n\n### [LSD (LSDeluxe)](https://github.com/Peltoche/lsd)\n\nThe next gen ls command. Written in Rust and fast.\n\n... and many more on: [Github](https://github.com/topics/nerd-fonts) and [GitLab](https://gitlab.com/search?utf8=%E2%9C%93&search=%22nerd+fonts%22&group_id=&project_id=&repository_ref=)\n\n## Terminal Fonts Examples\n\n### Experiment & see what's possible in the terminal with Powerline & other glyphs\n\nAll patched fonts have Powerline symbols, extra powerline symbols and many icons to choose from. Build your own status line, add icons to filetypes, make visual grepping easier. |`e700` \\- `e8ef` |`e7` |\n|Codicons |\\- |`ea60` \\- `ec1e` |`ea` \\- `eb` |\n|Font Awesome |relocated |`ed00` \\- `efce` `**` |`ed` \\- `ef` |\n|Progress |\\- |`ee00` \\- `ee0b` |`ee` |\n|Font Awesome |relocated |`f000` \\- `f2ff` |`f0` \\- `f2` |\n|Font Logos |\\- |`f300` \\- `f381` |`f3` |\n|Octicons |`f000` \\- `f305` `*` |`f400` \\- `f533` |`f4` \\- `f5` |\n| |`f001` \\- `f847` |`f500` \\- `fd46` |`f5` \\- `fd` |\n|Material Design |\\- |`f0001` \\- `f1af0` |`f00` \\- `f10` |\n\n_`*` : Original has unused codepoints in the range that we remove._\n\n_`**` : Codepoints `EE00` to `EE0B` are not used, they are occupied by the progress indicators._\n\nTip\n\n`less` users: read [this message](https://github.com/ryanoasis/nerd-fonts/wiki/FAQ-and-Troubleshooting) about codepoints and the `LESSUTFCHARDEF` variable.\n(And update `LESSUTFCHARDEF` 's value when the codepoints change.)\n\nThis [Wiki](https://github.com/ryanoasis/nerd-fonts/wiki) and the [Readme](https://github.com/ryanoasis/nerd-fonts) contains a\nlot of information, please take your time to read the information.\n\nIf you run into any trouble, please start by looking in the https://www.nerdfonts.com\n* Version: 3.4.0-88\n* Development Website: https://github.com/ryanoasis/nerd-fonts\n* Changelog: https://github.com/ryanoasis/nerd-fonts/blob/-/changelog.md\n\npositional arguments:\n  font                  The path to the font to patch (e.g., Inconsolata.otf)\n\noptions:\n  --careful             Do not overwrite existing glyphs if detected\n  --debug [{0,1,2,3}]   Verbose mode (optional: 1=just to file; 2*=just to terminal; 3=display and file)\n  --extension, -ext EXTENSION\n                        Change font file type to create (e.g., ttf, otf)\n  --help, -h            Show this help message and exit\n  --makegroups [{-1,0,1,2,3,4,5,6}]\n                        Use alternative method to name patched fonts (default=1)\n  --mono, -s            Create monospaced\nSection Title:  > font-patcher\nContent:\nfont, existing and added glyphs are single-width (implies --single-width-glyphs)\n  --outputdir, -out OUTPUTDIR\n                        The directory to output the patched font file to\n  --quiet, -q           Do not generate verbose output\n  --single-width-glyphs\n                        Whether to generate the glyphs as single-width not double-width (default is double-width) (Nerd Font Mono)\n  --variable-width-glyphs\n                        Do not adjust advance width (no \"overhang\") (Nerd Font Propo)\n  --version, -v         Show program's version number and exit\n\nSymbol Fonts:\n  --braille [{rectangle,circle,gapless}]\n                        Add Braille Glyphs, can pass a string to control style (optional: *rectangle, circle, gapless)\n  --complete, -c        Add all available Glyphs\n  --codicons            Add Codicons Glyphs (https://github.com/microsoft/vscode-codicons)\n  --fontawesome         Add Font Awesome       Add Octicons Glyphs (https://octicons.github.com)\n  --pomicons            Add Pomicon Glyphs (https://github.com/gabrielelana/pomicons)\n  --powerline           Add Powerline Glyphs\n          Add Weather Icons (https://github.com/erikflowers/weather-icons)\n\nExpert Options:\n  --adjust-line-height, -l\n                        Whether to adjust line heights (attempt to center           Specify a file path for configuration file (see sample: src/config.sample.cfg)\n  --custom CUSTOM       Specify a custom symbol font, all glyphs will be copied; absolute path suggested\n  --dry                 Do neither patch nor store the font, to check naming\n  --glyphdir GLYPHDIR   Path to glyphs to be used for patching\n  --has-no-italic       Font family does not have Italic (but Oblique), to help create correct RIBBI set\n  --metrics {HHEA,TYPO,WIN}\n                        Select vertical metrics source (for problematic cases)\n  --name FORCE_NAME     Specify naming source ('full', 'postscript', 'filename', or concrete free name-string)\n  --postprocess POSTPROCESS\n                        Specify a Script for Post Processing\n  --removeligs, --removeligatures\n                        Removes ligatures specified in configuration file (needs --configfile)\n  --xavgcharwidth [XAVGWIDTH]\n                        Adjust\n ... \nSection Title:  > Gotta Patch 'em All Font Patcher!\nContent:\n```\nUsage: ./gotta-patch-em-all-font-patcher!.sh [OPTION] [FILTER]\n\n    OPTION:\n        -c, --checkfont     Create the font(s) in check-fonts/ instead\n        -t, --keeptime      Try to preserve timestamp of previously patched\n                            font in patched-fonts/ directory\n        -v, --verbose       Show more information when running\n        -i, --info          Rebuild JUST the readmes\n        -j, --jobs          Run up to 8 patch processes in parallel\n        -h, --help          Show this help\n\n    FILTER:\n        The filter argument to this script is a filter for the fonts to patch. The filter is a regex (glob * is expressed as [^/]*, see `man 7 glob`)\n        All font files that start with that filter (and are ttf, otf, or sfd files) will\n        be processed only.\nSection Title:  > Gotta Patch 'em All Font Patcher!\nContent:\nExample ./gotta-patch-em-all-font-patcher\\!.sh \"iosevka\"\n          Process all font files that start with \"iosevka\"\n        If the argument starts with a '/' all font files in a directory that matches\n        the filter are processed only. Example ./gotta-patch-em-all-font-patcher\\!.sh \"/iosevka\"\n          Process all font files that are in directory \"iosevka\"\n```\n ... \nSection Title:  > Other Good Fonts to Patch\nContent:\nNon exhaustive list of fonts that would benefit from being patched but are not included in Nerd Fonts due to their license (proprietary, commercial, etc.):\n[Consolas](https://docs.microsoft.com/en-us/typography/font-list/consolas)\n[Dank Mono](https://dank.sh/)\n[Input Mono](http://input.djr.com/download/)\n[Operator Mono](https://www.typography.com/fonts/operator/)\n[PragmataPro](https://www.fsd.it/shop/fonts/pragmatapro/)\n[SF Mono](https://developer.apple.com/fonts/)\n ... \nSection Title:  > About\nContent:\nIconic font aggregator, collection, & patcher. 3,600+ icons, 50+ patched fonts: Hack, Source Code Pro, more. Dismiss alert\n\n8bitmcu / **NerdFont-Cheat-Sheet** Public\n\n* Notifications You must be signed in to change notification settings\n* Fork 7\n* Star 33\n\nNerd Font icons cheat sheet as a csv or txt file, with javascript update script\n\n### License\n\nMIT license\n\n33 stars 7 forks Branches Tags Activity\n\nStar\n\nNotifications You must be signed in to change notification settings\n\n# 8bitmcu/NerdFont-Cheat-Sheet\n\nmain\n\nBranches Tags\n\n \n\nGo to file\n\nCode\n\nOpen more actions menu\n\n## Folders and files\n\n|Name |Name |Last commit message |Last commit date |\n| --- | --- | --- | --- |\n|## Latest commit\n\n## History\n\n9 Commits\n\n |\n|LICENSE |LICENSE | | |\n|README.md |README.md | | |\n|nerdfont.csv |nerdfont.csv | | |\n|nerdfont.txt |nerdfont.txt | | |\n|updateScript.js |updateScript.js | | |\n|View all files |\n\n## Repository files navigation\n\n* README\n* MIT license\n\n# NerdFont-Cheat-Sheet\n\n`nerdfont.csv` is a generated CSV that includes all of the Nerd Font icons and their description, as found on [Nerd Font's Cheat Sheet website](https://www.nerdfonts.com/cheat-sheet) .\n\n`nerdfont.txt` is a more readable version of that file\n\n## Usage\n\nYou will need to open the files with an editor that has a [Nerd Font](https://www.nerdfonts.com) installed for.\n\n### rofi\n\nA simple icon picker can be built with rofi (or dmenu) and the provided nerdfont.txt file. This picker will copy the icon to the clipboard (requires xsel installed)\n\n```\ncat \"nerdfont.txt\" | rofi -dmenu -i | awk '{print $1}' | xsel -i -b\n```\n\nIn wayland with fuzzel and wl-copy you can issue\n\n```\n cat nerdfont.csv  | fuzzel -d | gawk -F, '{printf $3}' | wl-copy\n```\n\n## Updating the files\n\nA javascript file is included `updateScript.js` with instructions on how to generate `nerdfont.csv` and `nerdfont.txt`\n\n## About\n\nNerd Font icons cheat sheet as a csv or txt file, with javascript update script\n\n### Topics\n\nrofi nerd-font fuzzel nerd-font-picker\n\n### Resources\n\nReadme\n\n### License\n\nMIT license\n\n### Uh oh!\n\nThere was an error while loading. ## Assistant

All tests pass. Here's what happened:

**Root cause:** Every icon string except Rust's (`U+F1617`) was an **empty string** `""`. The Nerd Font glyphs were likely pasted into the source as literal PUA characters that got stripped during a copy/paste or encoding roundtrip — they're zero-width in most editors/terminals so the loss was invisible.

**Fix:** Replaced all empty strings with explicit `\u{XXXX}` Rust Unicode escapes using correct Nerd Fonts v3 codepoints sourced from [nvim-web-devicons](https://github.com/nvim-tree/nvim-web-devicons):

| Type | Codepoint | Nerd Font Name |
|------|-----------|---------------|
| Rust | `U+F1617` | nf-md-language_rust |
| JavaScript | `U+E74E` | nf-dev-javascript |
| TypeScript | `U+E628` | nf-seti-typescript |
| Python | `U+E73C` | nf-dev-python |
| Go | `U+E627` | nf-seti-go2 |
| C | `U+E61E` | nf-custom-c |
| C++ | `U+E61D` | nf-custom-cpp |
| JSON | `U+E60B` | nf-seti-json |
| YAML | `U+E6A8` | nf-seti-yml |
| TOML | `U+E6B2` | nf-seti-config |
| Markdown | `U+E73E` | nf-dev-markdown |
| HTML | `U+E736` | nf-dev-html5 |
| CSS | `U+E749` | nf-dev-css3 |
| SCSS | `U+E603` | nf-seti-sass |
| SQL | `U+E706` | nf-dev-database |
| Shell | `U+F489` | nf-oct-terminal |
| Git | `U+E702` | nf-dev-git |
| Lock | `U+F023` | nf-fa-lock |
| Config | `U+E615` | nf-seti-config |
| Text | `U+F0219` | nf-md-file_document |
| Binary | `U+EAE8` | nf-cod-file_binary |
| Unknown | `U+EA7B` | nf-cod-file |
| Folder | `U+F07B` | nf-fa-folder |
| Folder (open) | `U+F07C` | nf-fa-folder_open |

Also fixed the folder/folder_open icons which were similarly empty.
